# Fixed-average development experiment

Exposed IID development evidence only, not fresh confirmation, a formal no-harm guarantee, or default promotion. One globally fixed 0.5 raw/FE probability average is predeclared for every dataset, seed and fold. There is no OOF learning, learned-weight arm or optimizer. Outer-test labels are used only for scoring. Modes/runs must not be pooled or selected post hoc.

Scope: **canonical_nine**. Mode: **fixed_average**. Full design: nine families, 81 physical bundles, 243 outcomes.
Source changed: False; identity invalid: False; interrupted: False. Root error: None.
Outcome rows and outer folds are not independent datasets. Subset/nondefault-seed smoke is not the full study.
LightGBM only; seeds 42/137/2029; one global fixed 0.5 probability average, no OOF or optimizer.
Onehot categorical encoding, q0/q1 robust clipping, identity targets; candidates/additions 1000/50, repeats 3.
Reported FE/average/blend width is the final full-FE plan width, not an OOF feature union or ensemble width.
Compact provenance retains full-plan names, member/full plan/schema/state hashes, selected parameters, three inner-trial losses and partition counts/hashes; it contains no models, rows or vocabularies.

All three outcomes reuse the same full raw/FE fitted objects. Two complete procedures perform eight pipeline fits total (three inner candidates and one full refit each); there are no OOF fits or optimizer. Standalone pipeline times overlap and are not additive physical cost. physical_seconds counts each bundle once, including retries. Cumulative active_seconds in manifest.json includes preparation, workers, reports and archives. The ten-second cleanup reserve is not a hard bound; overshoot is reported.

## Requested three-arm coverage

| Method | Status counts |
|---|---|
| baseline-robust | {"success": 81} |
| featcopilot-frequency-aligned-robust | {"success": 81} |
| prediction-average-robust | {"success": 81} |

## Equal-family primary loss versus raw robust

| Method | Complete/requested families | Mean relative loss reduction |
|---|---|---|
| baseline-robust | 9/9 | 0.0 |
| featcopilot-frequency-aligned-robust | 9/9 | 0.047473478518555684 |
| prediction-average-robust | 9/9 | 0.06817522356676901 |
